HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000516/B004","root_000516/B009","root_001536/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_148a48b8e3c140948441","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:9:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\",\"B004\",\"B009\"]],[\"87:9:3\",\"root_001536\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:9:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\",\"B004\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dille açıkça iletilen öğüt, korunmuş okumanın başka bir kişinin iç dünyasında yeniden hatırlamaya dönüşmesini sağlayan aktarım halkasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5794e45f65258f01f066","text":"3. **The reminder tests receptivity rather than guaranteeing universal benefit.** Lexical evidence: نفع B001 benefit opposite harm; خشي B001 fear/awe in 87:10; جنب B003 avoidance in 87:11. Structural cue: conditional \"if it benefits\" is followed by two opposed responses: remembering and avoidance. Assigned role: benefit is realized in the fearful hearer, not in the avoidant one. Abductive move: the packet supplies benefit and response contrast; I infer selective efficacy. Reading change: the ayah does not make benefit automatic; it marks the threshold where reminder meets disposition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_699175c452d8132dd887","text":"87:10 supplied the expected internal conversion, but 87:11–14 was the stronger retrospective correction: benefit is visible only against the alternate route through avoidance, fire, suspended life, and then the counter-route of purification. The condition therefore acquired trajectory-changing force rather than remaining a weak usefulness qualifier.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_75534a05ba3f0350969b","text":"87:10-11 immediately filled the conditional: benefit appears in the one who fears, while avoidance marks non-benefit. 87:18-19 later made الذكرى surprisingly documentary, as though public reminder reactivates earlier صحف.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7d11a118827441b5ae97","text":"1. **Reminder as beneficial activation of retained recitation.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B003 bringing to mind and B009 reminder/admonition; نفع B001 benefit; قرء B001 recitation and نسي B001 forgetting in 87:6. Structural cue: \"remind\" follows the promise of non-forgetting. Assigned role: preserved recitation is converted into useful recall for others. Abductive move: the packet supplies retention and benefit; I infer that the addressee's secured memory becomes public admonition. Reading change: the command is conditional deployment of preserved speech.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_84b9d515dd0e08e63055","text":"4. **Reminder as rights-document activation.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B008 rights document/legal instrument; صحف B002 written sheets in 87:18-19. Structural cue: the surah ends with written sheets after repeated ذكر. Assigned role: الذكرى may function like a claim-bearing record recited into the present. Abductive move: the packet supplies legal document and scriptures; I infer that reminder carries an archived right/claim. Reading change: reminder becomes not only memory but summons by a recorded obligation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_922c9946d8b609b5f1d1","text":"2. **Public mention from preserved memory.** Lexical evidence: ذ ك ر B004 (mention on the tongue), ق ر ء B001/B002 in 87:6, ن س ي B001 (forgetting). Structural cue: anti-forgetting enables the imperative \"remind.\" Assigned role: private retention becomes public utterance. Abductive move: stable internal memory is the condition for outward mention. Reading change: the ayah is a transmission step from divine teaching to human address.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_92fc28e04024971693d6","text":"1. **Benefit is the conversion of prompting into recollection.** `ذ ك ر/B009` gives reminder or admonition, `ذ ك ر/B003` gives bringing a thing to mind, and `ن ف ع/B001` gives benefit opposed to harm. Repetition places the benefit verb between an external imperative and its nominal product; 87:10 then supplies the recipient’s `سَيَذَّكَّرُ`. The first occurrence emits a prompt and the second names its recoverable presence. I infer that benefit consists in successful conversion between those stages. The ayah reads as a feedback mechanism rather than a generic command plus condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_001536/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_935a48d8ea2a0daf76a1","text":"1. **Reminder as beneficial yield.** Lexical evidence: ذ ك ر B003/B009 (bringing to mind; admonition), ن ف ع B001 (benefit opposite harm), ر ع ي B001 in 87:4 (pasture), خ ر ج B003 (yield paid out). Structural cue: reminder follows pasture/yield imagery and divine recitation. Assigned role: ذكرى is the usable nourishment extracted from recited speech. Abductive move: admonition is like pasture: it matters by the benefit it produces in a receiver. Reading change: the conditional is practical, not hesitant; remind where the reminder can function.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b32d985c9c7f8063f249","text":"2. **Open mention seeks a hidden result.** `ذ ك ر/B004` is mention on the tongue, activated by `ج ه ر/B001` in 87:7, while `ذ ك ر/B003` supplies internal recall and `خ ف ي/B001` the concealed domain. The command’s role is audible output; the repeated noun points toward inward re-presence. I infer that the stated benefit is hidden uptake, not volume of delivery. The reading distinguishes public reminder from the private event that makes it useful.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_da79215a49303abdcb64","text":"87:18-19 made the documentary branch of ذ ك ر unexpectedly strong: the reminder is not an improvised speech but a claim preserved in former sheets. 87:4-5 also made \"benefit\" read through pasture versus refuse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B009","root_001536/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dcaee2b74b261e329671","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:9:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B009\"]],[\"87:9:3\",\"root_001536\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:9:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yarar, yalnız hoş bir etki değil, kişiyi kaçınma ve ateş yolundan anma ve arınma yoluna çevirebilen sonuçtur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e78111c4ea412b09d523","text":"3. **Rights document / former sheets activation.** Lexical evidence: ذ ك ر B008 (rights document), ص ح ف B002/B003 in 87:18-19, ء و ل B001/B002 (former/return to final meaning). Structural cue: reminder near the middle is later said to be in earlier sheets. Assigned role: ذكرى can behave like a documented claim on the listener. Abductive move: a reminder carries an older right/obligation into the present. Reading change: \"remind\" becomes serving notice of an already-inscribed claim.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B008","root_000516/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ec20ff6acf0615feb2d7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:9:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\",\"B009\"]],[\"87:9:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Öğüt, önceki sayfalarda kayıtlı bir hakkı bugünde yeniden yürürlüğe koyan belge niteliği de kazanabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f038da9e7f64063f114a","text":"2. **Mention on the tongue as public manifestation.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B004 verbal mention; جهر B001 open voicing in 87:7; علم B002 distinguishing mark/sign. Structural cue: the ayah follows \"He knows the open.\" Assigned role: reminder gives hidden knowledge a voiced marker. Abductive move: the packet supplies tongue-mention and open voicing; I infer that reminder is the sanctioned public form of what was preserved inwardly. Reading change: \"remind\" becomes making the sign audible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001536/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fae9cc6170be616b5bd1","text":"3. **Benefit is trajectory-changing rather than merely pleasant.** `ن ف ع/B001` is benefit as the opposite of harm. In 87:10–14, reminder opens onto recollection and purification or onto avoidance, fire, and suspended life. These contrasted sequences give the branch a causal role: useful reminder redirects a person between materially different outcomes. I infer the redirection arrow; the packet lists the responses and outcomes without explicitly making this verse their sole cause. `نَّفَعَتِ` therefore carries consequential rescue, not marginal advantage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
